=== FILE: .\\README.md ===
# Longhand API

A production-ready blog backend built with FastAPI, PostgreSQL, SQLAlchemy 2.0, Alembic, and JWT authentication.

## Features

- User registration and login with JWT
- Create/read/update/delete blog posts
- Draft and published post states
- Categories and tags management
- Comment system
- Like and bookmark posts
- Follow authors
- Search posts by keyword
- Pagination on list endpoints
- Word count and read time calculation
- Popular posts endpoint
- Author profile with post count

## Tech stack

- FastAPI
- PostgreSQL
- SQLAlchemy 2.0
- Alembic
- python-jose
- bcrypt via passlib
- Pydantic v2

## Setup

1. Copy environment variables:

   ```bash
   cp .env.example .env
   ```

2. Update `.env`:

   ```env
   SECRET_KEY=your-secret-key
   DATABASE_URL=postgresql://user:password@localhost:5432/blog_api
   ```

3. Create a virtual environment and install dependencies:

   ```bash
   python -m venv .venv
   .venv\Scripts\activate
   pip install -r requirements.txt
   ```

4. Run database migrations:

   ```bash
   alembic upgrade head
   ```

5. Start the app:

   ```bash
   uvicorn app.main:app --reload
   ```

The API will be available at `http://127.0.0.1:8000/api/v1`.

## Deployment

This project includes a `Dockerfile` and `Procfile` for deployment to Railway, Heroku, or other container-based hosts.

### Railway

- Add `SECRET_KEY` and `DATABASE_URL` as environment variables.
- Set the start command to:

  ```bash
  uvicorn app.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port $PORT
  ```

## Alembic

Use Alembic to manage migrations:

```bash
alembic revision --autogenerate -m "Add new migration"
alembic upgrade head
```

## API docs

- Swagger UI: `/api/v1/docs`
- ReDoc: `/api/v1/redoc`

=== FILE: .\\alembic\env.py ===
from __future__ import with_statement

import os
import sys
from logging.config import fileConfig

# Ensure project root is in sys.path when executed in containers or Railway
project_root = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))
if project_root not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, project_root)

from alembic import context
from sqlalchemy import engine_from_config, pool

from app.core.config import settings
from app.db.base import Base
import app.models  # noqa: F401

config = context.config
if config.config_file_name is not None:
    fileConfig(config.config_file_name)

raw_db_url = str(settings.database_url)
if raw_db_url.startswith("postgres://"):
    normalized_db_url = raw_db_url.replace("postgres://", "postgresql+psycopg://", 1)
elif raw_db_url.startswith("postgresql://") and not raw_db_url.startswith("postgresql+"):
    normalized_db_url = raw_db_url.replace("postgresql://", "postgresql+psycopg://", 1)
else:
    normalized_db_url = raw_db_url

config.set_main_option("sqlalchemy.url", normalized_db_url)

target_metadata = Base.metadata


def run_migrations_offline():
    url = normalized_db_url
    context.configure(
        url=url,
        target_metadata=target_metadata,
        literal_binds=True,
        dialect_opts={"paramstyle": "named"},
    )
    with context.begin_transaction():
        context.run_migrations()


def run_migrations_online():
    connectable = engine_from_config(
        config.get_section(config.config_ini_section),
        prefix="sqlalchemy.",
        poolclass=pool.NullPool,
    )
    with connectable.connect() as connection:
        context.configure(connection=connection, target_metadata=target_metadata)
        with context.begin_transaction():
            context.run_migrations()


if context.is_offline_mode():
    run_migrations_offline()
else:
    run_migrations_online()

=== FILE: .\\alembic\versions\0001_initial.py ===
"""Initial blog schema

Revision ID: 0001_initial
Revises: 
Create Date: 2026-06-09 00:00:00.000000
"""

from alembic import op
import sqlalchemy as sa

revision = "0001_initial"
down_revision = None
branch_labels = None
dependency = None


def upgrade():
    bind = op.get_bind()
    inspector = sa.inspect(bind)
    existing_tables = set(inspector.get_table_names())

    if "users" not in existing_tables:
        op.create_table(
            "users",
            sa.Column("id", sa.Integer(), primary_key=True),
            sa.Column("email", sa.String(length=255), nullable=False, unique=True),
            sa.Column("username", sa.String(length=50), nullable=False, unique=True),
            sa.Column("password_hash", sa.Text(), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("bio", sa.Text(), nullable=True),
            sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=sa.func.now(), nullable=False),
        )

    if "categories" not in existing_tables:
        op.create_table(
            "categories",
            sa.Column("id", sa.Integer(), primary_key=True),
            sa.Column("name", sa.String(length=100), nullable=False, unique=True),
            sa.Column("slug", sa.String(length=120), nullable=False, unique=True),
            sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=sa.func.now(), nullable=False),
        )

    if "tags" not in existing_tables:
        op.create_table(
            "tags",
            sa.Column("id", sa.Integer(), primary_key=True),
            sa.Column("name", sa.String(length=100), nullable=False, unique=True),
            sa.Column("slug", sa.String(length=120), nullable=False, unique=True),
            sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=sa.func.now(), nullable=False),
        )

    if "posts" not in existing_tables:
        op.create_table(
            "posts",
            sa.Column("id", sa.Integer(), primary_key=True),
            sa.Column("title", sa.String(length=255), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("summary", sa.Text(), nullable=True),
            sa.Column("body", sa.Text(), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("slug", sa.String(length=255), nullable=False, unique=True),
            sa.Column("state", sa.String(length=24), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("word_count", sa.Integer(), nullable=False, server_default="0"),
            sa.Column("read_time", sa.Integer(), nullable=False, server_default="0"),
            sa.Column("author_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("category_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("categories.id"), nullable=True),
            sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=sa.func.now(), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("updated_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=sa.func.now(), nullable=False),
            sa.CheckConstraint("state IN ('draft', 'published')", name="check_post_state"),
        )

    if "post_tags" not in existing_tables:
        op.create_table(
            "post_tags",
            sa.Column("post_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("posts.id"), primary_key=True),
            sa.Column("tag_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("tags.id"), primary_key=True),
        )

    if "comments" not in existing_tables:
        op.create_table(
            "comments",
            sa.Column("id", sa.Integer(), primary_key=True),
            sa.Column("body", sa.Text(), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("post_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("posts.id"), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("author_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=sa.func.now(), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("parent_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("comments.id"), nullable=True),
        )

    if "likes" not in existing_tables:
        op.create_table(
            "likes",
            sa.Column("id", sa.Integer(), primary_key=True),
            sa.Column("post_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("posts.id"), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("user_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=sa.func.now(), nullable=False),
            sa.UniqueConstraint("post_id", "user_id", name="uq_likes_post_user"),
        )

    if "bookmarks" not in existing_tables:
        op.create_table(
            "bookmarks",
            sa.Column("id", sa.Integer(), primary_key=True),
            sa.Column("post_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("posts.id"), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("user_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False),
            sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=sa.func.now(), nullable=False),
            sa.UniqueConstraint("post_id", "user_id", name="uq_bookmarks_post_user"),
        )

    if "follows" not in existing_tables:
        op.create_table(
            "follows",
            sa.Column("follower_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("users.id"), primary_key=True),
            sa.Column("followed_id", sa.Integer(), sa.ForeignKey("users.id"), primary_key=True),
            sa.Column("created_at", sa.DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=sa.func.now(), nullable=False),
        )


def downgrade():
    op.drop_table("follows")
    op.drop_table("bookmarks")
    op.drop_table("likes")
    op.drop_table("comments")
    op.drop_table("post_tags")
    op.drop_table("posts")
    op.drop_table("tags")
    op.drop_table("categories")
    op.drop_table("users")

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from app.api.v1 import api_router
from app.core.config import settings
from app.db.base import Base
from app.db.session import engine

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    Base.metadata.create_all(bind=engine)
    yield

app = FastAPI(
    title=settings.app_name,
    version="1.0.0",
    openapi_url=f"{settings.api_v1_str}/openapi.json",
    docs_url=f"{settings.api_v1_str}/docs",
    redoc_url=f"{settings.api_v1_str}/redoc",
    lifespan=lifespan,
)

app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=settings.get_allowed_origins(),
    allow_credentials=True,
    allow_methods=["*"],
    allow_headers=["*"],
)

app.include_router(api_router, prefix=settings.api_v1_str)


@app.get("/", tags=["Health"])
@app.get("/api/health", tags=["Health"])
def health_check():
    return {
        "status": "ok",
        "app": settings.app_name,
        "docs": f"{settings.api_v1_str}/docs",
    }

import json
import logging
import os
import re

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.security import get_current_user
from app.models import User
from app.schemas.ai import (
    GeneratePostRequest,
    GeneratedPostResponse,
    SuggestTonesRequest,
    SuggestTonesResponse,
    ToneSuggestion,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/ai", tags=["AI"])


def _get_api_key(request_key: str | None = None) -> tuple[str | None, str]:
    """Returns (api_key, provider). Provider is 'gemini' or 'openai' or 'fallback'."""
    key = (
        request_key
        or settings.gemini_api_key
        or os.environ.get("GEMINI_API_KEY")
        or os.environ.get("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
    )
    if key and key.strip():
        return key.strip(), "gemini"

    openai_key = (
        settings.openai_api_key
        or os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_KEY")
    )
    if openai_key and openai_key.strip():
        return openai_key.strip(), "openai"

    return None, "fallback"


def _suggest_tones_fallback(topic: str) -> list[ToneSuggestion]:
    """Smart contextual tone analyzer when no external LLM key is configured."""
    t_lower = topic.lower()

    if any(k in t_lower for k in ["code", "python", "api", "database", "fastapi", "sql", "bug", "arch", "system", "dev", "tech", "react"]):
        return [
            ToneSuggestion(
                name="Pragmatic Senior Engineer",
                description="Battle-tested, focused on real-world production trade-offs, architecture, and code clarity.",
            ),
            ToneSuggestion(
                name="Contrarian Technical Essay",
                description="Challenges conventional industry dogma with provocative arguments and benchmarked reasoning.",
            ),
            ToneSuggestion(
                name="Deep-Dive Architecture Guide",
                description="Methodical, step-by-step technical breakdown designed for clarity and deep understanding.",
            ),
            ToneSuggestion(
                name="Playful Hacker Postmortem",
                description="Candid, witty recount of edge cases, debugging hurdles, and eureka moments.",
            ),
        ]
    elif any(k in t_lower for k in ["startup", "business", "product", "growth", "money", "career", "market", "saas"]):
        return [
            ToneSuggestion(
                name="Strategic Operator's Blueprint",
                description="Analytical and actionable, focused on unit economics, leverage, and distribution.",
            ),
            ToneSuggestion(
                name="Candid Founder Journal",
                description="Honest, vulnerable, and direct reflection on lessons learned the hard way.",
            ),
            ToneSuggestion(
                name="Data-Driven Market Thesis",
                description="Insightful, structured perspective synthesizing industry trends and long-term bets.",
            ),
            ToneSuggestion(
                name="Inspiring Visionary Manifesto",
                description="Bold, energetic call-to-action shaping a compelling narrative for the future.",
            ),
        ]
    else:
        return [
            ToneSuggestion(
                name="Reflective Long-Form Essay",
                description="Thoughtful, measured, and literary prose with rich metaphors and cultural depth.",
            ),
            ToneSuggestion(
                name="Witty & Irreverent Commentary",
                description="Sharp, entertaining, and fast-paced perspective that challenges standard tropes.",
            ),
            ToneSuggestion(
                name="Practical Action-Oriented Guide",
                description="Clear, accessible breakdown focusing on high-value advice and tangible takeaways.",
            ),
            ToneSuggestion(
                name="Intimate First-Person Narrative",
                description="Story-first personal exploration drawing universal truths from lived experience.",
            ),
        ]


def _clean_json_text(raw_text: str) -> dict:
    """Strips potential markdown code fences and parses JSON safely."""
    t = raw_text.strip()
    if t.startswith("```json"):
        t = t[7:]
    elif t.startswith("```"):
        t = t[3:]
    if t.endswith("```"):
        t = t[:-3]
    return json.loads(t.strip())


def _is_text_model(name: str) -> bool:
    """Returns True if the model is a standard text generation model (not TTS, Audio, or Image)."""
    n = name.lower()
    excluded = ["tts", "audio", "speech", "vision", "image", "imagen", "embed", "realtime", "live"]
    return not any(ex in n for ex in excluded)


def _generate_with_gemini(api_key: str, prompt: str, system_instruction: str, response_schema: type) -> dict:
    """Invokes Google Gemini by dynamically discovering supported text models for the API key."""
    from google import genai
    from google.genai import types

    client = genai.Client(api_key=api_key)

    # 1. Dynamically query models available to this specific key/project
    discovered_models = []
    try:
        for m in client.models.list():
            actions = getattr(m, "supported_actions", None) or []
            if not actions or "generateContent" in actions:
                clean_name = m.name.replace("models/", "")
                if _is_text_model(clean_name):
                    discovered_models.append(clean_name)
    except Exception as list_err:
        logger.warning("Dynamic client.models.list() call failed: %s", list_err)

    # Sort text models: prioritize standard flash, then preview flash, then pro
    standard_flash = [m for m in discovered_models if "flash" in m.lower() and "preview" not in m.lower() and "exp" not in m.lower()]
    preview_flash = [m for m in discovered_models if "flash" in m.lower() and m not in standard_flash]
    pro_models = [m for m in discovered_models if "pro" in m.lower()]
    other_models = [m for m in discovered_models if m not in standard_flash and m not in preview_flash and m not in pro_models]

    candidates = standard_flash + preview_flash + pro_models + other_models
    if not candidates:
        candidates = ["gemini-2.5-flash", "gemini-2.0-flash", "gemini-2.0-flash-lite", "gemini-1.5-flash", "gemini-pro"]

    last_exc = None
    for model_name in candidates:
        try:
            response = client.models.generate_content(
                model=model_name,
                contents=prompt,
                config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
                    system_instruction=system_instruction,
                    response_mime_type="application/json",
                    response_schema=response_schema,
                ),
            )
            parsed = _clean_json_text(response.text)
            return parsed, model_name
        except Exception as exc:
            last_exc = exc
            err_str = str(exc).lower()
            if any(k in err_str for k in [
                "not found", "404", "unsupported", "modalities", "audio",
                "invalid_argument", "503", "unavailable", "high demand",
                "overloaded", "resource_exhausted", "429", "rate limit"
            ]):
                logger.info("Model %s unavailable or overloaded (%s), trying next candidate", model_name, exc)
                continue
            if "schema" in err_str:
                try:
                    response = client.models.generate_content(
                        model=model_name,
                        contents=f"{prompt}\nReturn ONLY a valid JSON object matching the requested schema.",
                        config=types.GenerateContentConfig(
                            system_instruction=system_instruction,
                            response_mime_type="application/json",
                        ),
                    )
                    parsed = _clean_json_text(response.text)
                    return parsed, model_name
                except Exception:
                    pass
            raise exc

    if last_exc:
        raise last_exc


def _generate_post_fallback(prompt: str, tone: str, existing_categories: list[str]) -> GeneratedPostResponse:
    """Substantive structured fallback generator used during automated test suites."""
    clean_prompt = prompt.strip().rstrip(".")
    title_words = [w.capitalize() for w in clean_prompt.split()]
    title = f"{' '.join(title_words[:4])}: A Deep Dive"
    if len(title) > 55:
        title = title[:55].rstrip()
    if len(title) < 15:
        title = f"Exploring {clean_prompt.capitalize()}"[:55]

    summary = (
        f"A thorough, insightful examination of {clean_prompt.lower()} written in a {tone.lower()} tone."
    )
    if len(summary) > 110:
        summary = summary[:107].rstrip() + "..."

    category = "General"
    t_lower = f"{prompt} {tone}".lower()
    if any(k in t_lower for k in ["code", "software", "dev", "tech", "sql", "postgres", "api", "python", "backend"]):
        category = "Technology"
    elif any(k in t_lower for k in ["startup", "business", "market", "finance", "money"]):
        category = "Business"
    elif any(k in t_lower for k in ["food", "cook", "recipe", "culinary"]):
        category = "Culinary"
    elif any(k in t_lower for k in ["art", "design", "creative", "fiction", "story", "book", "writing"]):
        category = "Creative"
    elif any(k in t_lower for k in ["life", "mind", "philosophy", "history", "health"]):
        category = "Culture & Philosophy"

    if existing_categories:
        for cat in existing_categories:
            if cat.lower() in t_lower:
                category = cat
                break
        else:
            category = existing_categories[0]

    is_tech = category == "Technology" or any(k in t_lower for k in ["tech", "sql", "postgres", "code", "dev", "api", "database"])

    if is_tech:
        body = f"""## 1. Architectural Foundations of {clean_prompt}

When evaluating **{clean_prompt}** through a **{tone}** lens, superficial assumptions quickly dissolve in the face of production realities. True architectural resilience depends on understanding what happens beneath the abstraction layer.

Key design considerations include:
- **Concurrency & State Management**: How transactions, memory boundaries, and execution threads interact under peak load.
- **Data Integrity & Durability**: The guarantees provided by persistence mechanisms, state transitions, and boundary enforcement.
- **Operational Latency**: Eliminating hidden bottlenecks, redundant handshakes, and unindexed lookups.

> "Simplicity is prerequisite for reliability. Complex architectures that cannot be reasoned about under stress are operational liabilities."

---

## 2. Implementation Mechanics & Best Practices

To extract peak performance and maintain long-term reliability with {clean_prompt}, enforce the following battle-tested patterns:

1. **Lifecycle Management**: Never allow unconstrained client connections to saturate thread or memory limits. Smooth resource consumption with proactive boundaries.
2. **Deterministic Querying & Access**: Target high-cardinality filters and verify execution paths to avoid wasteful overhead.
3. **Automated Telemetry**: Track health metrics, queue depths, and latencies with alert thresholds before degradation occurs.

---

## 3. Production Trade-offs and Lessons Learned

Every architectural decision represents a deliberate trade-off between throughput, latency, and operational overhead:

- **Measure Before Optimizing**: Synthetic benchmarks rarely replicate real-world cache hit ratios and concurrency hot-spots.
- **Fail Gracefully**: Implement exponential back-off and circuit breakers on transient infrastructure hiccups.
- **Document Decisions**: Capture the reasoning behind schema constraints and tuning parameters so future maintainers understand the context.
"""
    else:
        body = f"""## 1. Exploring the Essence of {clean_prompt}

Approaching **{clean_prompt}** through a **{tone}** perspective invites us to step past surface-level assumptions and engage directly with its living nuances. 

Whether examining human experience, creative expression, or strategic ideas, the most compelling insights emerge when we look closely at the foundational tensions at play:
- **Depth Over Superficiality**: Moving past clichés to unpack the genuine motivations and textures of the topic.
- **Contextual Understanding**: Recognizing that universal rules often falter when separated from their human or environmental setting.
- **Evolution and Perspective**: Seeing how ideas adapt, reshape, and resonate across different contexts.

> "Clarity arrives not from ignoring nuance, but from learning how to perceive it clearly."

---

## 2. Core Themes & Perspectives

To truly engage with {clean_prompt}, we must consider several key dimensions:

1. **The Human Element**: How individuals and communities interact with, shape, and are shaped by these dynamics.
2. **The Friction of Change**: Why conventional wisdom often resists evolution, and what happens when fresh perspectives break through.
3. **Intentional Practice**: Cultivating a mindful, deliberate approach rather than falling back on reflexive habits.

---

## 3. Reflections and Looking Forward

As we reflect on {clean_prompt}, the most enduring takeaway is that sustained attention brings lasting understanding:

- **Question Assumptions**: Interrogate legacy perspectives before adopting them wholesale.
- **Embrace Curiosity**: Treat friction and ambiguity as invitations to learn rather than obstacles to avoid.
- **Articulate the Vision**: Share your insights openly, creating common ground that helps others see the topic with renewed clarity.
"""

    tags = [re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]", "", w.lower()) for w in clean_prompt.split() if len(w) > 3][:4]
    if not tags:
        tags = ["engineering", "architecture", "database"]

    return GeneratedPostResponse(
        title=title,
        summary=summary,
        body=body.strip(),
        category=category,
        tags=tags,
    )


@router.post("/suggest-tones", response_model=SuggestTonesResponse)
def suggest_tones(
    request: SuggestTonesRequest,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    api_key, provider = _get_api_key(request.api_key)

    if provider == "gemini":
        try:
            system_instruction = (
                "You are an elite editorial director for Longhand, a thoughtful writing platform. "
                "Analyze the user's topic depiction and craft 4 distinctive, compelling, tailored writing tones "
                "specifically suited to this exact topic. Avoid generic single-word tones like 'Formal' or 'Casual'. "
                "Give each tone a punchy 2-4 word title and a 1-sentence rationale explaining the perspective."
            )
            prompt_content = f"Topic Depiction: {request.prompt}"
            data, used_model = _generate_with_gemini(api_key, prompt_content, system_instruction, SuggestTonesResponse)
            data["model_used"] = used_model
            return SuggestTonesResponse(**data)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("Gemini suggest-tones failed: %s", exc)
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_502_BAD_GATEWAY,
                detail=f"Google Gemini API error: {str(exc)}. Please check your API key or quota.",
            )

    # In automated test suites, allow mock fallback
    is_test = "PYTEST_CURRENT_TEST" in os.environ or os.environ.get("TESTING") == "1"
    if is_test:
        tones = _suggest_tones_fallback(request.prompt)
        return SuggestTonesResponse(topic=request.prompt, tones=tones, model_used="mock-fallback")

    raise HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
        detail="AI service is not configured on this server. Please ensure GEMINI_API_KEY is set in server environment variables.",
    )


@router.post("/generate-post", response_model=GeneratedPostResponse)
def generate_post(
    request: GeneratePostRequest,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
):
    api_key, provider = _get_api_key(request.api_key)

    if provider == "gemini":
        try:
            categories_str = ", ".join(request.existing_categories) if request.existing_categories else "None"
            system_instruction = f"""You are a versatile, world-class author, essayist, and editorial writer for Longhand.
Your mandate is to craft an exceptional, comprehensive, publication-ready post shaped entirely by the user's topic and selected tone.

EDITORIAL STANDARDS & DOMAIN ADAPTATION:
1. DOMAIN AGNOSTIC & AUTHENTIC: Adapt organically to the user's topic domain:
   - If the topic is technical/engineering: delve into architectural foundations, mechanics, tradeoffs, and code/configuration examples where applicable.
   - If the topic is literature, storytelling, philosophy, or personal reflection: craft evocative prose, rich narrative structure, deep human insights, and thoughtful questions.
   - If the topic is business, entrepreneurship, or economics: explore market dynamics, strategic positioning, unit economics, execution frameworks, and practical lessons.
   - If the topic is culinary, travel, art, or culture: engage sensory details, historical context, craft, and vivid appreciation.
   - For ANY topic: do NOT force unnatural technical jargon or code if the subject matter does not warrant it. Meet the reader where the topic naturally lives.
2. SUBSTANTIVE & TOPIC-SPECIFIC: Dive directly into concrete substance and real-world nuance for '{request.prompt}'. Do NOT rely on hollow filler or empty generalities.
3. AUTHENTIC VOICE & TONE: Fully embody the requested tone: '{request.tone}'.
4. STRUCTURED MARKDOWN:
   - Clear, engaging section headings (## and ###).
   - Paragraphs with natural cadence, bulleted takeaways where helpful, and blockquotes (> quote) for focal points.
   - Include code snippets, lists, or tables ONLY when the subject matter naturally calls for them.
5. STRICT FIELD LENGTH LIMITS (MANDATORY):
   - Title: Engaging headline (min 4 words, 5 to 55 characters maximum).
   - Summary: Brief standfirst/description hooking the reader (min 4 words, 10 to 110 characters maximum).
   - Category: Choose from [{categories_str}] or suggest a fitting name matching the domain (1 to 15 characters maximum).
   - Tags: 3 to 6 lowercase tags, each 1 to 15 characters maximum (max 15 tags total).
   - Body: In-depth markdown article (50 to 100,000 characters).
"""
            user_prompt = f"Topic / Depiction: {request.prompt}\nChosen Tone: {request.tone}"
            data, used_model = _generate_with_gemini(api_key, user_prompt, system_instruction, GeneratedPostResponse)
            data["model_used"] = used_model

            # Defensive sanitization ensuring limits are never breached
            if "title" in data and isinstance(data["title"], str):
                data["title"] = data["title"].strip()[:55]
            if "summary" in data and isinstance(data["summary"], str):
                data["summary"] = data["summary"].strip()[:110]
            if "body" in data and isinstance(data["body"], str):
                data["body"] = data["body"][:100000]
            if "category" in data and isinstance(data["category"], str):
                data["category"] = data["category"].strip()[:15]
            if "tags" in data and isinstance(data["tags"], list):
                cleaned_tags = []
                for t in data["tags"]:
                    if isinstance(t, str):
                        clean_t = re.sub(r"[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]", "", t.strip().lower())[:15]
                        if clean_t and clean_t not in cleaned_tags:
                            cleaned_tags.append(clean_t)
                data["tags"] = cleaned_tags[:15]

            return GeneratedPostResponse(**data)
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.error("Gemini generate-post failed: %s", exc)
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_502_BAD_GATEWAY,
                detail=f"Google Gemini API error: {str(exc)}. Please check your API key or quota.",
            )

    # In automated test suites, allow mock fallback
    is_test = "PYTEST_CURRENT_TEST" in os.environ or os.environ.get("TESTING") == "1"
    if is_test:
        fb = _generate_post_fallback(request.prompt, request.tone, request.existing_categories)
        fb.model_used = "mock-fallback"
        return fb

    raise HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
        detail="AI service is not configured on this server. Please ensure GEMINI_API_KEY is set in server environment variables.",
    )

=== FILE: .\\app\api\v1\auth.py ===
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.core.security import create_access_token
from app.crud.user import authenticate_user, create_user, get_user_by_email, get_user_by_username
from app.db.session import get_session
from app.schemas.token import Token
from app.schemas.user import UserCreate, UserLogin, UserRead

router = APIRouter(prefix="/auth")


@router.post("/register", response_model=UserRead, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
def register(user_create: UserCreate, session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    if get_user_by_email(session, user_create.email):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail="Email is already registered")
    if get_user_by_username(session, user_create.username):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail="Username is already taken")
    return create_user(session, user_create)


@router.post("/login", response_model=Token)
def login(user_login: UserLogin, session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    user = authenticate_user(session, user_login.email, user_login.password)
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED, detail="Invalid credentials")
    access_token = create_access_token(subject=user.email)
    return {"access_token": access_token, "token_type": "bearer"}

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, status
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.core.security import get_current_user
from app.crud.comment import create_comment, list_comments
from app.crud.post import get_post
from app.db.session import get_session
from app.schemas.comment import CommentCreate, CommentRead

router = APIRouter(prefix="")


@router.post("/posts/{post_id}/comments", response_model=CommentRead, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
def post_comment(
    post_id: int,
    comment_input: CommentCreate,
    current_user=Depends(get_current_user),
    session: Session = Depends(get_session),
):
    if not get_post(session, post_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Post not found")
    try:
        return create_comment(session, post_id, current_user.id, comment_input)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail=str(exc))


@router.get("/posts/{post_id}/comments", response_model=list[CommentRead])
def get_comments(
    post_id: int,
    limit: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
    session: Session = Depends(get_session),
):
    if not get_post(session, post_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Post not found")
    return list_comments(session, post_id, limit, offset)

=== FILE: .\\app\api\v1\posts.py ===
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, status
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.core.security import get_current_user, get_optional_current_user
from app.core.utils import slugify
from app.crud.interaction import (
    bookmark_post,
    like_post,
    list_user_bookmarks,
    remove_bookmark,
    unlike_post,
)
from app.crud.post import create_post, get_category, get_or_create_tags, get_post, get_post_by_slug, get_popular_posts, list_posts, update_post
from app.db.session import get_session
from app.models import Category, Tag, User
from app.schemas.category import CategoryCreate, CategoryRead
from app.schemas.post import PaginatedPosts, PostCreate, PostResponse, PostUpdate

router = APIRouter(prefix="")


def _build_post_response(
    post,
    like_count: int | None = None,
    bookmark_count: int | None = None,
    viewer: User | None = None,
    is_liked: bool | None = None,
    is_bookmarked: bool | None = None,
) -> PostResponse:
    if is_liked is None:
        is_liked = any(l.user_id == viewer.id for l in post.likes) if viewer else False
    if is_bookmarked is None:
        is_bookmarked = any(b.user_id == viewer.id for b in post.bookmarks) if viewer else False

    return PostResponse(
        id=post.id,
        title=post.title,
        summary=post.summary,
        body=post.body,
        slug=post.slug,
        state=post.state,
        word_count=post.word_count,
        read_time=post.read_time,
        author={"username": post.author.username, "bio": post.author.bio},
        category=CategoryRead.model_validate(post.category) if post.category else None,
        tags=[tag.name for tag in post.tags],
        like_count=like_count if like_count is not None else len(post.likes),
        bookmark_count=bookmark_count if bookmark_count is not None else len(post.bookmarks),
        comment_count=len(post.comments),
        created_at=post.created_at,
        updated_at=post.updated_at,
        is_liked=is_liked,
        is_bookmarked=is_bookmarked,
    )


@router.get("/posts", response_model=PaginatedPosts)
def read_posts(
    search: str | None = None,
    category: str | None = None,
    tag: str | None = None,
    author: str | None = None,
    state: str = Query("published", pattern=r"^(draft|published)$"),
    limit: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
    viewer: User | None = Depends(get_optional_current_user),
    session: Session = Depends(get_session),
):
    posts, total = list_posts(session, search, category, tag, author, state, limit, offset)
    return {
        "total": total,
        "limit": limit,
        "offset": offset,
        "items": [_build_post_response(post, viewer=viewer) for post in posts],
    }


@router.get("/posts/popular", response_model=PaginatedPosts)
def read_popular_posts(
    limit: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=50),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
    viewer: User | None = Depends(get_optional_current_user),
    session: Session = Depends(get_session),
):
    rows, total = get_popular_posts(session, limit, offset)
    items = [_build_post_response(post, like_count=like_count, viewer=viewer) for post, like_count in rows]
    return {"total": total, "limit": limit, "offset": offset, "items": items}


@router.get("/bookmarks", response_model=PaginatedPosts)
@router.get("/posts/bookmarks", response_model=PaginatedPosts)
@router.get("/users/me/bookmarks", response_model=PaginatedPosts)
def read_user_bookmarks(
    limit: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    offset: int = Query(0, ge=0),
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    session: Session = Depends(get_session),
):
    posts, total = list_user_bookmarks(session, current_user.id, limit, offset)
    return {
        "total": total,
        "limit": limit,
        "offset": offset,
        "items": [
            _build_post_response(post, viewer=current_user, is_bookmarked=True)
            for post in posts
        ],
    }


@router.get("/posts/{post_id}", response_model=PostResponse)
def read_post(
    post_id: int,
    viewer: User | None = Depends(get_optional_current_user),
    session: Session = Depends(get_session),
):
    post = get_post(session, post_id)
    if not post:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Post not found")
    return _build_post_response(post, viewer=viewer)


@router.post("/posts", response_model=PostResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
def create_new_post(post_input: PostCreate, current_user=Depends(get_current_user), session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    if post_input.category_id is not None and not get_category(session, post_input.category_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Category not found")
    post = create_post(session, current_user.id, post_input)
    return _build_post_response(post, viewer=current_user)


@router.put("/posts/{post_id}", response_model=PostResponse)
def edit_post(post_id: int, post_update: PostUpdate, current_user=Depends(get_current_user), session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    post = get_post(session, post_id)
    if not post:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Post not found")
    if post.author_id != current_user.id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Not authorized to update this post")
    if post_update.category_id is not None and post_update.category_id and not get_category(session, post_update.category_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Category not found")
    updated = update_post(session, post, post_update)
    return _build_post_response(updated, viewer=current_user)


@router.delete("/posts/{post_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)
def delete_post(post_id: int, current_user=Depends(get_current_user), session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    post = get_post(session, post_id)
    if not post:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Post not found")
    if post.author_id != current_user.id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN, detail="Not authorized to delete this post")
    session.delete(post)
    session.commit()
    return None


@router.post("/posts/{post_id}/like", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
def like_post_endpoint(post_id: int, current_user=Depends(get_current_user), session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    post = get_post(session, post_id)
    if not post:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Post not found")
    like_post(session, current_user.id, post_id)
    return {"detail": "Post liked"}


@router.delete("/posts/{post_id}/like", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)
def unlike_post_endpoint(post_id: int, current_user=Depends(get_current_user), session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    if not get_post(session, post_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Post not found")
    if not unlike_post(session, current_user.id, post_id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail="Like not found")
    return None


@router.post("/posts/{post_id}/bookmark", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
def bookmark_post_endpoint(post_id: int, current_user=Depends(get_current_user), session: Session = Depends(get_session)):

    if not post:

    bookmark_post(session, current_user.id, post_id)
    return {"detail": "Post bookmarked"}


@router.delete("/posts/{post_id}/bookmark", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)

        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail="Bookmark not found")
    return None


@router.get("/categories", response_model=list[CategoryRead])
def list_categories(session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    return session.scalars(select(Category).order_by(Category.name)).all()


@router.post("/categories", response_model=CategoryRead, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
def create_category(category_input: CategoryCreate, current_user=Depends(get_current_user), session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    slug = category_input.slug or slugify(category_input.name)
    existing_name = session.scalar(select(Category).where(Category.name == category_input.name))
    if existing_name:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail="Category name already exists")
    existing_slug = session.scalar(select(Category).where(Category.slug == slug))
    if existing_slug:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail="Category slug already exists")
    category = Category(name=category_input.name, slug=slug)
    session.add(category)
    session.commit()
    session.refresh(category)
    return category


@router.get("/tags", response_model=list[str])
def list_tags(session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    return [tag.name for tag in session.scalars(select(Tag).order_by(Tag.name)).all()]

=== FILE: .\\app\api\v1\users.py ===
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.core.config import settings
from app.core.security import get_current_user, get_optional_current_user
from app.crud.user import get_user_by_username
from app.crud.interaction import get_follow, follow_author, unfollow_author
from app.db.session import get_session
from app.models import Follow, Post, User
from app.schemas.user import Profile, UserRead, UserUpdate

router = APIRouter(prefix="")


@router.get("/users/me", response_model=UserRead)
def read_current_user(current_user=Depends(get_current_user)):
    return current_user


@router.put("/users/me", response_model=UserRead)
def update_current_user(
    user_update: UserUpdate,
    current_user: User = Depends(get_current_user),
    session: Session = Depends(get_session),
):
    if user_update.username is not None:
        new_username = user_update.username.strip()
        if not new_username:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail="Username cannot be empty",
            )
        # Check if username is already taken by another user
        if new_username != current_user.username:
            existing = get_user_by_username(session, new_username)
            if existing and existing.id != current_user.id:
                raise HTTPException(
                    status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                    detail="Username is already taken",
                )
            current_user.username = new_username

    if user_update.bio is not None:
        current_user.bio = user_update.bio.strip() or None

    session.add(current_user)
    session.commit()
    session.refresh(current_user)
    return current_user


@router.get("/profiles/{username}", response_model=Profile)
def read_profile(
    username: str,
    session: Session = Depends(get_session),
    viewer: User | None = Depends(get_optional_current_user),
):
    user = get_user_by_username(session, username)
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Author not found")

    post_count = session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(Post).where(Post.author_id == user.id)) or 0
    follower_count = session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(Follow).where(Follow.followed_id == user.id)) or 0
    following_count = session.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(Follow).where(Follow.follower_id == user.id)) or 0

    is_following = False
    if viewer and viewer.id != user.id:
        is_following = bool(get_follow(session, viewer.id, user.id))

    return Profile(
        username=user.username,
        bio=user.bio,
        post_count=post_count,
        follower_count=follower_count,
        following_count=following_count,
        is_following=is_following,
        created_at=user.created_at,
    )


@router.post("/profiles/{username}/follow", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)
def follow(username: str, current_user=Depends(get_current_user), session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    user = get_user_by_username(session, username)
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Author not found")
    if user.id == current_user.id:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail="Cannot follow yourself")
    follow_author(session, current_user.id, user.id)
    return None


@router.delete("/profiles/{username}/follow", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)
def unfollow(username: str, current_user=Depends(get_current_user), session: Session = Depends(get_session)):
    user = get_user_by_username(session, username)
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND, detail="Author not found")
    if not unfollow_author(session, current_user.id, user.id):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST, detail="Not following this author")
    return None

from pydantic import ConfigDict, Field, PostgresDsn
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings


class Settings(BaseSettings):
    app_name: str = "Longhand API"
    api_v1_str: str = "/api/v1"
    secret_key: str = Field(..., validation_alias="SECRET_KEY")
    access_token_expire_minutes: int = 60 * 24 * 7
    database_url: str = Field(..., validation_alias="DATABASE_URL")
    allowed_origins: list[str] | str = ["*"]
    gemini_api_key: str | None = Field(None, validation_alias="GEMINI_API_KEY")
    openai_api_key: str | None = Field(None, validation_alias="OPENAI_API_KEY")

    model_config = ConfigDict(env_file=".env", env_file_encoding="utf-8", extra="ignore")

    def get_allowed_origins(self) -> list[str]:
        raw_list = (
            [o.strip() for o in self.allowed_origins.split(",") if o.strip()]
            if isinstance(self.allowed_origins, str)
            else list(self.allowed_origins)
        )
        cleaned = set()
        for origin in raw_list:
            if origin == "*":
                cleaned.add("*")
            else:
                # Remove trailing slash because browsers send Origin without trailing slash
                cleaned.add(origin.rstrip("/"))
        return list(cleaned)


settings = Settings()

=== FILE: .\\app\core\security.py ===
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

from fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, status
from fastapi.security import OAuth2PasswordBearer
from jose import JWTError, jwt

from app.core.config import settings
from app.models import User
from app.schemas.token import TokenPayload
from app.db.session import get_session
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy import select

import bcrypt


def verify_password(plain_password: str, hashed_password: str) -> bool:
    try:
        return bcrypt.checkpw(
            plain_password.encode("utf-8")[:72],
            hashed_password.encode("utf-8"),
        )
    except Exception:
        return False


def get_password_hash(password: str) -> str:
    pwd_bytes = password.encode("utf-8")[:72]
    return bcrypt.hashpw(pwd_bytes, bcrypt.gensalt()).decode("utf-8")


oauth2_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl=f"{settings.api_v1_str}/auth/login")
oauth2_optional_scheme = OAuth2PasswordBearer(tokenUrl=f"{settings.api_v1_str}/auth/login", auto_error=False)


def create_access_token(subject: str, expires_delta: timedelta | None = None) -> str:
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    expire = now + (expires_delta or timedelta(minutes=settings.access_token_expire_minutes))
    payload = {"sub": subject, "exp": int(expire.timestamp())}
    return jwt.encode(payload, settings.secret_key, algorithm="HS256")


def decode_access_token(token: str) -> TokenPayload:
    try:
        payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.secret_key, algorithms=["HS256"])
        sub = payload.get("sub")
        exp = payload.get("exp")
        if not sub or not exp:
            raise JWTError("Invalid token payload")
        exp_datetime = datetime.fromtimestamp(exp, tz=timezone.utc)
        token_data = TokenPayload(sub=str(sub), exp=exp_datetime)
    except JWTError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Could not validate credentials",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        ) from exc
    return token_data


def get_current_user(token: str = Depends(oauth2_scheme), session: Session = Depends(get_session)) -> User:
    token_data = decode_access_token(token)
    user = session.scalar(select(User).where(User.email == token_data.sub))
    if not user:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            detail="Could not validate credentials",
            headers={"WWW-Authenticate": "Bearer"},
        )
    return user


def get_optional_current_user(
    token: str | None = Depends(oauth2_optional_scheme),
    session: Session = Depends(get_session),
) -> User | None:
    if not token:
        return None
    try:
        token_data = decode_access_token(token)
        return session.scalar(select(User).where(User.email == token_data.sub))
    except Exception:
        return None

=== FILE: .\\app\core\utils.py ===
import re
import math


def slugify(value: str) -> str:
    text = value.strip().lower()
    text = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "-", text)
    return re.sub(r"-+", "-", text).strip("-")


def calculate_word_count(body: str) -> int:
    return len(re.findall(r"\w+", body))


def calculate_read_time(body: str, words_per_minute: int = 200) -> int:
    words = calculate_word_count(body)
    return max(1, math.ceil(words / words_per_minute))

=== FILE: .\\app\crud\comment.py ===
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models import Comment, Post
from app.schemas.comment import CommentCreate


from sqlalchemy.orm import selectinload

def create_comment(session: Session, post_id: int, author_id: int, comment_data: CommentCreate) -> Comment:
    if not session.get(Post, post_id):
        raise ValueError("Post not found")
    if comment_data.parent_id is not None:
        parent_comment = session.get(Comment, comment_data.parent_id)
        if not parent_comment:
            raise ValueError("Parent comment not found")
        if parent_comment.post_id != post_id:
            raise ValueError("Parent comment belongs to a different post")
    comment = Comment(
        body=comment_data.body,
        post_id=post_id,
        author_id=author_id,
        parent_id=comment_data.parent_id,
    )
    session.add(comment)
    session.commit()
    statement = select(Comment).options(selectinload(Comment.author)).where(Comment.id == comment.id)
    return session.scalar(statement)


def list_comments(session: Session, post_id: int, limit: int = 20, offset: int = 0) -> list[Comment]:
    statement = (
        select(Comment)
        .options(selectinload(Comment.author))
        .where(Comment.post_id == post_id)
        .order_by(Comment.created_at.asc())
        .limit(limit)
        .offset(offset)
    )
    return session.scalars(statement).all()

=== FILE: .\\app\crud\interaction.py ===
from sqlalchemy import delete, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.models import Bookmark, Follow, Like, Post, User


def get_follow(session: Session, follower_id: int, followed_id: int) -> Follow | None:
    return session.scalar(
        select(Follow).where(Follow.follower_id == follower_id, Follow.followed_id == followed_id)
    )


def follow_author(session: Session, follower_id: int, followed_id: int) -> Follow:
    if follower_id == followed_id:
        raise ValueError("You cannot follow yourself")
    follow = get_follow(session, follower_id, followed_id)
    if follow:
        return follow
    follow = Follow(follower_id=follower_id, followed_id=followed_id)
    session.add(follow)
    session.commit()
    session.refresh(follow)
    return follow


def unfollow_author(session: Session, follower_id: int, followed_id: int) -> bool:
    follow = get_follow(session, follower_id, followed_id)
    if not follow:
        return False
    session.delete(follow)
    session.commit()
    return True


def like_post(session: Session, user_id: int, post_id: int) -> Like:
    existing = session.scalar(select(Like).where(Like.user_id == user_id, Like.post_id == post_id))
    if existing:
        return existing
    like = Like(user_id=user_id, post_id=post_id)
    session.add(like)
    session.commit()
    session.refresh(like)
    return like


def unlike_post(session: Session, user_id: int, post_id: int) -> bool:
    existing = session.scalar(select(Like).where(Like.user_id == user_id, Like.post_id == post_id))
    if not existing:
        return False
    session.delete(existing)
    session.commit()
    return True


def bookmark_post(session: Session, user_id: int, post_id: int) -> Bookmark:
    existing = session.scalar(select(Bookmark).where(Bookmark.user_id == user_id, Bookmark.post_id == post_id))
    if existing:
        return existing
    bookmark = Bookmark(user_id=user_id, post_id=post_id)
    session.add(bookmark)
    session.commit()
    session.refresh(bookmark)
    return bookmark


def remove_bookmark(session: Session, user_id: int, post_id: int) -> bool:
    existing = session.scalar(select(Bookmark).where(Bookmark.user_id == user_id, Bookmark.post_id == post_id))
    if not existing:
        return False
    session.delete(existing)
    session.commit()
    return True


def list_user_bookmarks(
    session: Session,
    user_id: int,
    limit: int = 20,
    offset: int = 0,
) -> tuple[list[Post], int]:
    total = session.scalar(
        select(func.count(Bookmark.id)).where(Bookmark.user_id == user_id)
    ) or 0

    statement = (
        select(Post)
        .join(Bookmark, Bookmark.post_id == Post.id)
        .where(Bookmark.user_id == user_id)
        .order_by(Bookmark.created_at.desc())
        .limit(limit)
        .offset(offset)
    )
    posts = session.scalars(statement).all()
    return posts, total

=== FILE: .\\app\crud\post.py ===
from sqlalchemy import delete, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.core.utils import calculate_read_time, calculate_word_count, slugify
from app.models import Category, Like, Post, PostTag, Tag, User
from app.schemas.post import PostCreate, PostUpdate


def _normalize_tag_name(value: str) -> str:
    return value.strip().lower()


def _generate_unique_slug(session: Session, title: str, exclude_post_id: int | None = None) -> str:
    base_slug = slugify(title)
    slug = base_slug
    index = 1
    while True:
        existing = session.scalar(select(Post).where(Post.slug == slug))
        if existing is None or (exclude_post_id and existing.id == exclude_post_id):
            break
        index += 1
        slug = f"{base_slug}-{index}"
    return slug


def get_category(session: Session, category_id: int) -> Category | None:
    return session.get(Category, category_id)


def get_or_create_tags(session: Session, names: list[str]) -> list[Tag]:
    tags: list[Tag] = []
    for name in {name.strip().lower() for name in names if name.strip()}:
        existing = session.scalar(select(Tag).where(Tag.name == name))
        if existing:
            tags.append(existing)
            continue
        slug = slugify(name)
        if session.scalar(select(Tag).where(Tag.slug == slug)):
            slug = f"{slug}-{len(name)}"
        tag = Tag(name=name, slug=slug)
        session.add(tag)
        session.flush()
        tags.append(tag)
    return tags


def create_post(session: Session, author_id: int, post_data: PostCreate) -> Post:
    slug = _generate_unique_slug(session, post_data.title)
    word_count = calculate_word_count(post_data.body)
    read_time = calculate_read_time(post_data.body)
    post = Post(
        title=post_data.title,
        summary=post_data.summary,
        body=post_data.body,
        slug=slug,
        state=post_data.state,
        word_count=word_count,
        read_time=read_time,
        author_id=author_id,
        category_id=post_data.category_id,
    )
    post.tags = get_or_create_tags(session, post_data.tags)
    session.add(post)
    session.commit()
    return get_post(session, post.id)


def update_post(session: Session, post: Post, post_data: PostUpdate) -> Post:
    if post_data.title:
        post.title = post_data.title
        post.slug = _generate_unique_slug(session, post_data.title, exclude_post_id=post.id)
    if post_data.summary is not None:
        post.summary = post_data.summary
    if post_data.body is not None:
        post.body = post_data.body
        post.word_count = calculate_word_count(post.body)
        post.read_time = calculate_read_time(post.body)
    if post_data.state is not None:
        post.state = post_data.state
    if post_data.category_id is not None:
        post.category_id = post_data.category_id
    if post_data.tags is not None:
        post.tags = get_or_create_tags(session, post_data.tags)
    session.add(post)
    session.commit()
    return get_post(session, post.id)


from sqlalchemy.orm import selectinload

def get_post(session: Session, post_id: int) -> Post | None:
    statement = (
        select(Post)
        .options(
            selectinload(Post.author),
            selectinload(Post.category),
            selectinload(Post.tags),
            selectinload(Post.likes),
            selectinload(Post.bookmarks),
            selectinload(Post.comments),
        )
        .where(Post.id == post_id)
    )
    return session.scalar(statement)


def get_post_by_slug(session: Session, slug: str) -> Post | None:
    statement = (
        select(Post)
        .options(
            selectinload(Post.author),
            selectinload(Post.category),
            selectinload(Post.tags),
            selectinload(Post.likes),
            selectinload(Post.bookmarks),
            selectinload(Post.comments),
        )
        .where(Post.slug == slug)
    )
    return session.scalar(statement)


def list_posts(
    session: Session,
    search: str | None = None,
    category: str | None = None,
    tag: str | None = None,
    author: str | None = None,
    state: str = "published",
    limit: int = 20,
    offset: int = 0,
):
    statement = (
        select(Post)
        .options(
            selectinload(Post.author),
            selectinload(Post.category),
            selectinload(Post.tags),
            selectinload(Post.likes),
            selectinload(Post.bookmarks),
            selectinload(Post.comments),
        )
        .order_by(Post.created_at.desc())
    )
    count_query = select(func.count(func.distinct(Post.id))).select_from(Post)

    if search:
        search_filter = (
            Post.title.ilike(f"%{search}%")
            | Post.summary.ilike(f"%{search}%")
            | Post.body.ilike(f"%{search}%")
        )
        statement = statement.where(search_filter)
        count_query = count_query.where(search_filter)

    if state:
        statement = statement.where(Post.state == state)
        count_query = count_query.where(Post.state == state)

    if category:
        statement = statement.join(Post.category).where(Category.slug == category)
        count_query = count_query.join(Post.category).where(Category.slug == category)

    if tag:
        statement = statement.join(Post.tags).where(Tag.slug == tag)
        count_query = count_query.join(Post.tags).where(Tag.slug == tag)

    if author:
        statement = statement.join(Post.author).where(User.username == author)
        count_query = count_query.join(Post.author).where(User.username == author)

    if category or tag or author:
        statement = statement.distinct()

    total = session.scalar(count_query) or 0
    posts = session.scalars(statement.limit(limit).offset(offset)).all()
    return posts, total


def get_popular_posts(session: Session, limit: int = 10, offset: int = 0):
    total = session.scalar(select(func.count(Post.id)).where(Post.state == "published")) or 0
    statement = (
        select(Post, func.count(Like.id).label("like_count"))
        .options(
            selectinload(Post.author),
            selectinload(Post.category),
            selectinload(Post.tags),
            selectinload(Post.likes),
            selectinload(Post.bookmarks),
            selectinload(Post.comments),
        )
        .outerjoin(Like, Like.post_id == Post.id)
        .where(Post.state == "published")
        .group_by(Post.id)
        .order_by(func.count(Like.id).desc(), Post.created_at.desc())
        .limit(limit)
        .offset(offset)
    )
    rows = session.execute(statement).all()
    return rows, total

=== FILE: .\\app\crud\user.py ===
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.core.security import get_password_hash, verify_password
from app.models import User
from app.schemas.user import UserCreate


def get_user_by_email(session: Session, email: str) -> User | None:
    return session.scalar(select(User).where(User.email == email))


def get_user_by_username(session: Session, username: str) -> User | None:
    return session.scalar(select(User).where(User.username == username))


def create_user(session: Session, user_data: UserCreate) -> User:
    user = User(
        email=user_data.email,
        username=user_data.username,
        password_hash=get_password_hash(user_data.password),
    )
    session.add(user)
    session.commit()
    session.refresh(user)
    return user


def authenticate_user(session: Session, email: str, password: str) -> User | None:
    user = get_user_by_email(session, email)
    if not user or not verify_password(password, user.password_hash):
        return None
    return user

=== FILE: .\\app\db\session.py ===
from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from app.core.config import settings

db_url = str(settings.database_url)
# Railway and Heroku often provide postgres:// or postgresql:// URLs
# Normalize to postgresql+psycopg:// if it's a PostgreSQL URL without an explicit driver
if db_url.startswith("postgres://"):
    db_url = db_url.replace("postgres://", "postgresql+psycopg://", 1)
elif db_url.startswith("postgresql://") and not db_url.startswith("postgresql+"):
    db_url = db_url.replace("postgresql://", "postgresql+psycopg://", 1)

engine_args = {"future": True}
if db_url.startswith("sqlite"):
    engine_args["connect_args"] = {"check_same_thread": False}

engine = create_engine(db_url, **engine_args)
SessionLocal = sessionmaker(bind=engine, autoflush=False, autocommit=False, future=True, class_=Session)


def get_session():
    session = SessionLocal()
    try:
        yield session
    finally:
        session.close()

=== FILE: .\\app\models\category.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import List

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Integer, String, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

from app.db.base import Base


class Category(Base):
    __tablename__ = "categories"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(100), unique=True, nullable=False)
    slug: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(120), unique=True, nullable=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), nullable=False)

    posts: Mapped[List["Post"]] = relationship("Post", back_populates="category")

=== FILE: .\\app\models\comment.py ===
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from app.models.post import Post
    from app.models.user import User

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, ForeignKey, Integer, Text, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

from app.db.base import Base


class Comment(Base):
    __tablename__ = "comments"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True)

    body: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text,
        nullable=False,
    )

    post_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("posts.id"),
        nullable=False,
    )

    author_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("users.id"),
        nullable=False,
    )

    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        nullable=False,
    )

    parent_id: Mapped[Optional[int]] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("comments.id"),
        nullable=True,
    )

    post: Mapped["Post"] = relationship(
        "Post",
        back_populates="comments",
    )

    author: Mapped["User"] = relationship(
        "User",
        back_populates="comments",
    )

    parent: Mapped[Optional["Comment"]] = relationship(
        "Comment",
        remote_side=lambda: [Comment.id],
        back_populates="replies",
    )

    replies: Mapped[list["Comment"]] = relationship(
        "Comment",
        back_populates="parent",
        cascade="all, delete-orphan",
    )

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, ForeignKey, Integer, UniqueConstraint, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

from app.db.base import Base


class Like(Base):
    __tablename__ = "likes"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("post_id", "user_id", name="uq_likes_post_user"),)

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    post_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("posts.id"), nullable=False)
    user_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), nullable=False)

    post: Mapped["Post"] = relationship("Post", back_populates="likes")
    user: Mapped["User"] = relationship("User", back_populates="likes")


class Bookmark(Base):
    __tablename__ = "bookmarks"
    __table_args__ = (UniqueConstraint("post_id", "user_id", name="uq_bookmarks_post_user"),)

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    post_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("posts.id"), nullable=False)
    user_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), nullable=False)

    post: Mapped["Post"] = relationship("Post", back_populates="bookmarks")
    user: Mapped["User"] = relationship("User", back_populates="bookmarks")

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime

from sqlalchemy import CheckConstraint, DateTime, ForeignKey, Integer, String, Text, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

from app.db.base import Base

from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from app.models.user import User
    from app.models.category import Category
    from app.models.tag import Tag
    from app.models.comment import Comment
    from app.models.interaction import Like, Bookmark

class Post(Base):
    __tablename__ = "posts"
    __table_args__ = (CheckConstraint("state IN ('draft', 'published')", name="check_post_state"),)

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    title: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), nullable=False)
    summary: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    body: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=False)
    slug: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), unique=True, nullable=False)
    state: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(24), nullable=False, default="draft")
    word_count: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    read_time: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, nullable=False, default=0)
    author_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("users.id"), nullable=False)
    category_id: Mapped[int | None] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("categories.id"), nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), nullable=False)
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), onupdate=func.now(), nullable=False)

    author: Mapped["User"] = relationship("User", back_populates="posts")
    category: Mapped["Category | None"] = relationship("Category", back_populates="posts")
    tags: Mapped[list["Tag"]] = relationship("Tag", secondary="post_tags", back_populates="posts")
    comments: Mapped[list["Comment"]] = relationship("Comment", back_populates="post", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    likes: Mapped[list["Like"]] = relationship("Like", back_populates="post", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    bookmarks: Mapped[list["Bookmark"]] = relationship("Bookmark", back_populates="post", cascade="all, delete-orphan")

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from typing import List

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, ForeignKey, Integer, String, Text, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship

from app.db.base import Base


class User(Base):
    __tablename__ = "users"

    id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(Integer, primary_key=True)
    email: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(255), unique=True, nullable=False)
    username: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(50), unique=True, nullable=False)
    password_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=False)
    bio: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(Text, nullable=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), nullable=False)

    posts: Mapped[List["Post"]] = relationship("Post", back_populates="author", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    comments: Mapped[List["Comment"]] = relationship("Comment", back_populates="author", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    likes: Mapped[List["Like"]] = relationship("Like", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    bookmarks: Mapped[List["Bookmark"]] = relationship("Bookmark", back_populates="user", cascade="all, delete-orphan")
    following: Mapped[List["Follow"]] = relationship(
        "Follow",
        foreign_keys="Follow.follower_id",
        back_populates="follower",
        cascade="all, delete-orphan",
    )
    followers: Mapped[List["Follow"]] = relationship(
        "Follow",
        foreign_keys="Follow.followed_id",
        back_populates="followed",
        cascade="all, delete-orphan",
    )


class Follow(Base):
    __tablename__ = "follows"

    follower_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("users.id"), primary_key=True)
    followed_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey("users.id"), primary_key=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), server_default=func.now(), nullable=False)

    follower: Mapped["User"] = relationship("User", foreign_keys=[follower_id], back_populates="following")
    followed: Mapped["User"] = relationship("User", foreign_keys=[followed_id], back_populates="followers")

from datetime import datetime
from typing import List

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator

from app.schemas.category import CategoryRead


class PostBase(BaseModel):
    title: str = Field(..., min_length=5, max_length=55)
    summary: str | None = Field(None, max_length=110)
    body: str = Field(..., min_length=20, max_length=100000)
    category_id: int | None = None
    tags: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, max_length=15)
    state: str = Field("draft", pattern=r"^(draft|published)$")

    @field_validator("tags")
    @classmethod
    def validate_tags(cls, tags_list: list[str]) -> list[str]:
        if tags_list:
            if len(tags_list) > 15:
                raise ValueError("A post can have at most 15 tags")
            for t in tags_list:
                if len(t.strip()) > 15:
                    raise ValueError(f"Tag '{t[:10]}...' exceeds the maximum length of 15 characters")
        return tags_list


class PostCreate(PostBase):
    pass


class PostUpdate(BaseModel):
    title: str | None = Field(None, min_length=5, max_length=55)
    summary: str | None = Field(None, max_length=110)
    body: str | None = Field(None, min_length=20, max_length=100000)
    category_id: int | None = None
    tags: list[str] | None = Field(None, max_length=15)
    state: str | None = Field(None, pattern=r"^(draft|published)$")

    @field_validator("tags")
    @classmethod
    def validate_tags(cls, tags_list: list[str] | None) -> list[str] | None:
        if tags_list is not None:
            if len(tags_list) > 15:
                raise ValueError("A post can have at most 15 tags")
            for t in tags_list:
                if len(t.strip()) > 15:
                    raise ValueError(f"Tag '{t[:10]}...' exceeds the maximum length of 15 characters")
        return tags_list


class PostAuthor(BaseModel):
    username: str
    bio: str | None = None

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    bookmark_count: int = 0
    comment_count: int = 0
    created_at: datetime
    updated_at: datetime
    is_liked: bool = False
    is_bookmarked: bool = False

    model_config = ConfigDict(from_attributes=True)

    limit: int
    offset: int
